--no-new-privs --bounding-set=-all /usr/bin/timeout -k 1 8 /usr/bin/bash -c 'rea

d -r before < /proc/pressure/cpu

> before=${before##*total=}

> pids=()

> for i in 1 2 3 4; do

>     timeout -k 1 3 bash -c '"'"'while :; do :; done'"'"' & pids+=("$!")

> done

> for pid in "${pids[@]}"; do wait "$pid" || :; done

> read -r after < /proc/pressure/cpu

> after=${after##*total=}

> [[ $before =~ ^[0-9]+$ && $after =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || exit 2

> ((after > before)) || exit 1

> printf '"'"'PSI-CRECIO-%s\n'"'"' "$((4+5))"

> '
